atha snānabhedāḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ svāmamucyate |
teṣāṃ madhye tu yagnityaṃ tat punarbhidyate tridhā ||

mandhyāmmānamubhābhyāntsnānadeśāḥ prakīrttitāḥ ||

bhaṅkhaḥ |
svānaṃ tu dvividhaṃ preokaṃ gaula - mukhya prabhedataḥ |
tayostu vāruṇaṃ mukhyaṃ tat punaḥ ṣaḍdhiṃ bhavet ||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ kriyāṅga maslakarṣaṇaṃ |
kriyātrānaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhaṃ ṣor̤ā snānaṃ prakīrtyate ||
eteṣāṃ krameṇa lakṣaṇānyāha ma eka
asnātastu pumānācī japā viśvanādiṣu |
prātaḥkhānaṃ tadarthava nityaṃ snānaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ||

marna kāryaṃ |

eke tattaddevānāṃ prajā hāmī tayā sarvādipaśupatyantānāṃ mapatrā-
kānāṃ dharāditanvayakānāñca prajā hāmī evaṃ kimedi-catu
viṃśatimantrānakā maṃhāre darśanārtha prathamāekaprānta
mantramādyatvenodāharati, 'śivāya matyamityādi | 'evamiti sṛṣṭi
(1) madhyasya ṣṭhalāī vācanātprādiśati |

1716
caturvargacintāmano pariśeṣagya gar̤e
[ 25 ba0 |
krameṇa prāgukavirithyādi - śivāntadevatā ekamantreṣu punaḥ saṃhārakame-
lāgniṃ śivāya mṛtyaṃ svāhetyādi brahmaṇe svāhetyantaṃ tathā paśupati-
patnyādimavantaṃ utakrameṇa saṃpūjya ghṛta-cam-mamitkramāt pratyekaṃ
hātavyaṃ | oṃ satyādi kevalājya homamantrastatra sarva dhagamiti vākya-
yānte bhuvaḥ svāheti tṛtīyaḥ, bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ svāheti caturthamantraḥ |
'evaṃ pṛthak pṛthagiti tathā śivasya patnotyete catvā gamantrā jahyāḥ |
evaṃ bhatra jalaṃ me chindhi jihvāyāṃ gmaṃ bhava me'ghaṃ jahi bhūḥ svāhe-
tyādibhirūhitaiścaturbhirmantrairheaumaḥ | tathā virajamantraiśca tathā prāṇe
niviṣṭa) ityādiṣaṇamantraiśra śrāddhokaḥ svagṭahyoḥ pita pitāmaha-
prapitāmahādyaddṛśe homaśca | evaṃ maptāhaṃ pratyahaṃ hatvā matame dine bhuvā-
diladevatādeśāt viprānabhyacyaṃ sarvādibhyo'ṣṭau piṇḍā dayāḥ | evaṃ
kṛte auvacchrāddhe svabāndhave mṛte nāśaucaṃ na mṛtakaṃ | evaṃ tasmādutpannaḥ
putro'pi pitrādijātakarmādinā saṃskārthaḥ ma ca jñāno bhavet |
evaṃ duhitā ca | tathācaitamantau yoginā jāyante iti |
iti śrīmahārājādhirāja śraumahādevoya-makalakaraṇādhipati-
paṇḍita - śrahamādriviracitaṃ caturvargacintāmaṇī pariśeṣa-
khaṇḍa śrāddhakalpa jauvacchrāddhaprayogaprakaraṇam
samāptam |
